# Commands · P · part 3

1,482 entries, platform-sync to PRD.template. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **platform-sync**: Review or push the FULL personal-OS platform — all four repos — with the correct git invocations for each. The platform is zdots (kernel) + adots (home-dir config, a BARE repo) + my (private, nested) + vdots (nvim). Use for "review the platform", "is everything pushed/in sync", "prepare the platform for push", or any cross-repo status/sync check. ([just3ws/zdots](https://github.com/just3ws/zdots/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/platform-sync.md) · ★1)

- **portaljs-add-geo**: Auto-ingest a geospatial file (GeoJSON, Shapefile, GeoPackage, KML/KMZ, FlatGeobuf, CSV-with-geometry) into a PortalJS portal — on your own machine, no server.
